**业务逻辑：** 1) 聚合 `m10_lab_qc`（`qc_result='FAIL'`）；2) `ratio = count / total_fail_count`；3) 按执行加工师统计 `fail_rate` 用于质量考核；4) 阈值告警：单加工师 `fail_rate > 5%` 标红。

#### API-38 重算日统计

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/lab/statistics/recalc
```

```json
{
  "stat_dates": ["2026-10-30", "2026-10-31"],
  "store_id": 1,
  "stat_types": ["OPERATOR", "KIND", "STORE"]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "TASK20261031120000",
    "accepted": true,
    "estimated_rows": 112,
    "message": "已提交异步重算，完成后通过 WebSocket 推送"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `m10:lab:stat:recalc` 可调用；2) 异步执行，`calc_version` 递增并覆盖同日同维度旧行；3) 最多一次重算 31 天；4) 结果通过 WebSocket `m10.lab.stat.recalc_done` 推送。

```json
        "qty_available": 0,
        "unit": "副",
        "unit_cost": 268.0,
        "amount": 804.00,
        "expiry_date": null,
        "supply_type": "STOCK",
        "issue_no": "ISS20261020001",
        "status": "AVAILABLE",
        "updated_at": "2026-10-31T09:12:00+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "total": 1,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 按批次粒度返回，命中 `idx_m10_stock_batch_sku`/`idx_m10_stock_batch_product`；2) `qty_available` 为数据库生成列，无需应用层计算；3) `unit_cost` 无 `m10:stock:cost` 权限则返回 null；4) `expiring_days` 过滤近效期。

#### API-42 补货领用申请

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/replenish/apply
```

```json
{
  "store_id": 1,
  "target_warehouse_id": 11,
  "items": [
    { "product_code": "OPLN000456", "sku_key": "OPLN000456｜-4.25｜-0.75｜180", "qty": 10 },
    { "product_code": "OPFR000123", "sku_key": "OPFR000123｜-｜-｜BLK", "qty": 5 }
  ],
  "apply_reason": "月度常规补货",
  "operator_id": 6001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "apply_no": "BH202610310001",
    "status": "PENDING_M15",
    "m15_issue_no": "ISS202610310005",
    "m15_request_status": "ACCEPTED",
    "line_count": 2,
    "applied_at": "2026-10-31T11:20:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验门店与目标二级库映射；2) 调 M15-04 `POST /internal/v1/m15/issue/prepare` 发起出库；3) 记录本地补货单（`apply_no`）与 `m15_issue_no` 映射；4) M15 出库失败返回 `"1007021"`；5) 补货单不直接改本子模块库存，等 M15 出库后再由 API-43 确认入库。

#### API-43 领用入库确认

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/replenish/BH202610310001/confirm
```

```json
{
  "m15_issue_no": "ISS202610310005",
  "receive_lines": [
    { "product_code": "OPLN000456", "sku_key": "OPLN000456｜-4.25｜-0.75｜180", "batch_no": "L20261028", "qty": 10, "unit_cost": 268.0 },
    { "product_code": "OPFR000123", "sku_key": "OPFR000123｜-｜-｜BLK", "batch_no": "IN20261028", "qty": 5, "unit_cost": 320.0 }
  ],
  "operator_id": 6001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "apply_no": "BH202610310001",
    "status": "RECEIVED",
    "batch_created": 2,
    "batch_updated": 0,
    "qty_added_total": 15,
    "movements": [
      { "movement_no": "LS20261031000201", "txn_type": "IN_ISSUE", "qty": 10, "after_qty": 13 },
      { "movement_no": "LS20261031000202", "txn_type": "IN_ISSUE", "qty": 5, "after_qty": 7 }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m15_issue_no` 存在且已出库；2) 回传数量与 M15 出库数量必须一致（`"1007022"`）；3) 按 `batch_key` 幂等：存在则累加 `qty_on_hand`，不存在则建批次行；4) 每行写一条 `IN_ISSUE` 流水（`after_qty = before_qty + qty`）；5) 全部在单一事务内完成。

#### API-44 报损登记

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/scrap
```

```json
{
  "store_id": 1,
  "batch_id": 10388,
  "qty": 1,
  "scrap_type": "DAMAGED",
  "reason": "搬运破损，外框变形不可修复",
  "approval_no": "SP202610310005",
  "operator_id": 6001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "movement_no": "LS20261031000210",
    "batch_id": 10388,
    "qty": 1,
    "before_qty": 5,
    "after_qty": 4,
    "batch_status": "AVAILABLE",
    "scrap_amount": 320.00
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `qty <= qty_on_hand`（`"1007050"`）；2) `reason` ≥5 字；3) 写 `OUT_SCRAP` 流水；4) 若报损后 `qty_on_hand=0` 且 `scrap_type='EXPIRED'/'DAMAGED'`，批次置 `EXPIRED`/`DAMAGED`；5) 金额超 500 元需审批单号；6) 不释放预占（预占先于报废时应先释放）。

#### API-47 录入盘点结果

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/checks/PD20261031001/enter
```

```json
{
  "lines": [
    { "line_no": 1, "batch_id": 10231, "actual_qty": 2, "reason_code": "UNKNOWN" },
    { "line_no": 2, "batch_id": 10388, "actual_qty": 6, "reason_code": "MISCOUNT_PREV" },
    { "line_no": 3, "batch_id": 10412, "actual_qty": 10, "reason_code": null }
  ],
  "submit": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "check_no": "PD20261031001",
    "status": "SUBMITTED",
    "entered_line_count": 1832,
    "diff_line_count": 7,
    "gain_qty_total": 2,
    "loss_qty_total": 6,
    "gain_amount": 320.00,
    "loss_amount": 706.00,
    "net_amount": -386.00,
    "need_approval": true,
    "approval_threshold": 500.00
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 盘点单必须 `CHECKING`（`enter` 第一次会自动从 `DRAFT` 转 `CHECKING`）；2) `actual_qty` 0~99999（`"1007024"`）；3) 有差异时必须给原因（`"1007025"`）；4) `submit=true` 时校验全部行已录（`"1007027"`）；5) 计算盘盈盘亏汇总，`need_approval` 由 `loss_amount > 500` 判定。

#### API-48 确认盘点差异并回传 M15

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/checks/PD20261031001/confirm
```

```json
{
  "approval_no": "SP202610310006",
  "confirm_remark": "10 月月度全盘，已核对货架与仓库",
  "operator_id": 5001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "check_no": "PD20261031001",
    "status": "CONFIRMED",
    "movements_created": 7,
    "m15_callback_status": "SUCCESS",
    "m15_callback_at": "2026-10-31T21:10:00+08:00",
    "m15_adjust_no": "ADJ202610310002",
    "total_gain_amount": 320.00,
    "total_loss_amount": 706.00
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 仅 `SUBMITTED` 可确认；2) 盘亏超阈值必须有审批单（`"1007028"`）；3) 写 `IN_CHECK`/`OUT_CHECK` 流水并更新批次数量；4) 调 M15-05 `POST /internal/v1/m15/stock/adjust` 回传差异（**M10 不直接改主库**）；5) 回传失败置 `m15_callback_status='FAILED'`，由 `job_m10_check_callback_retry` 每 5 分钟重试；6) 盘点范围批次解冻。

#### API-51 更新库存策略

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/stock/policies/301
```

```json
{
  "qty_safety": 2,
  "qty_reorder_point": 4,
  "reserve_ttl_minutes": 720,
  "reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes": 2880,
  "reserve_ttl_custom_minutes": 10080,
  "approval_loss_amount": 500.00,
  "version": 2
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 301,
    "version": 3,
    "batch_rows_updated": 1,
    "cache_invalidated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 乐观锁 `version` 校验（冲突 `40900`）；2) TTL 层级必须 普通 ≤ 加急 ≤ 定制 ≤ 10080（否则 `"1007017"`）；3) 安全库存变更后批量回写 `m10_stock_batch.qty_safety`（生成列自动重算 `qty_available`）；4) 失效 Redis 策略缓存与可售量缓存。

#### API-53 手工触发对账

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/reconcile/run
```

```json
{
  "reconcile_date": "2026-10-31",
  "store_id": 1,
  "reconcile_level": "SKU"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "TASK20261031113000",
    "reconcile_batch_no": "DZ2026103101",
    "accepted": true,
    "compared_scope": "store=1 level=SKU"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 每门店每日限 1 次手工触发（`"1007046"`）；2) 异步任务拉 M15 快照并逐 SKU 比对；3) `reconcile_batch_no` 按当日序号递增；4) 结果落 `m10_stock_reconcile`。

#### API-54 差异处理

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/stock/reconcile/90011/handle
```

```json
{
  "handle_type": "REISSUE",
  "root_cause": "UNSYNCED_SALE",
  "handle_remark": "10-30 售出 1 副，M15 出库接口超时导致未落库，已补录出库",
  "operator_id": 5001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 90011,
    "handle_status": "HANDLING",
    "handle_type": "REISSUE",
    "m15_issue_no": "ISS202610310009",
    "auto_resolve_check_at": "2026-11-01T01:30:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 超容差差异必须归因（`"1007029"`）；2) `REISSUE` 调 M15-04 补发出库；`RESYNC` 拉 M15 快照覆盖本地；`ADJUST` 生成盘点调整单；`ACCEPT` 直接 `IGNORED`；3) 处理成功但差异未归零时置 `HANDLING`，次日对账若归零自动转 `RESOLVED`；4) 修平必须写处理说明（`"1007030"`）。

### 6.7 内部契约接口（P-16 ~ P-20）

#### API-55 P-16 取加工单进度

> **契约编号 P-16，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `GET /internal/v1/m10/lab/jobs/{lab_job_no}`
> **调用方：** M16-04、M20（移动端查进度）、M22（报表）｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 100ms

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/lab/jobs/JG202610310011
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "lab_job_no": "JG202610310011",
    "order_no": "PO202610310011",
    "sales_order_no": "SO202610310011",
    "status": "PROCESSING",
    "status_m16": "PROCESSING",
    "progress_percent": 43,
    "current_step_seq": 2,
    "current_step_name": "装框",
    "step_count": 7,
    "completed_step_count": 3,
    "is_urgent": true,
    "is_overdue": false,
    "promised_at": "2026-10-31T16:00:00+08:00",
    "planned_finish_at": "2026-10-31T15:42:00+08:00",
    "started_at": "2026-10-31T09:02:00+08:00",
    "finished_at": null,
    "ready_at": null,
    "qc_status": "PENDING",
    "has_outsource": true,
    "outsource_in_transit": 0,
    "estimated_finish_at": "2026-10-31T15:42:00+08:00",
    "steps": [
      {
        "step_seq": 1,
        "process_name": "磨边",
        "process_type": "EDGING",
        "status": "QC_PASSED",
        "is_outsourced": false,
        "started_at": "2026-10-31T09:02:00+08:00",
        "finished_at": "2026-10-31T09:18:00+08:00",
        "actual_minutes": 18
      },
      {
        "step_seq": 2,
        "process_name": "装框",
        "process_type": "MOUNTING",
        "status": "PROCESSING",
        "is_outsourced": false,
        "started_at": "2026-10-31T09:22:00+08:00",
        "finished_at": null,
        "actual_minutes": null
      }

    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T09:22:00+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 只读，不修改任何状态；2) 单号不存在返回 `"1007001"`（HTTP 404）；3) `status_m16` 按共享规范 §13.1 映射（`IN_PRODUCTION`/`READY` 等财务态）；4) `steps` 精简版（不含加工师姓名，避免泄露内部人员信息给 M20 顾客端）；5) 走 Redis 缓存 `m10:lab:job:{lab_job_no}` TTL 10s，状态变更时主动删除。

#### API-56 P-17 收款完成触发派工

> **契约编号 P-17，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/on-order-paid`
> **调用方：** M16-04 ｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 200ms ｜ **幂等：** 必须带 `Idempotency-Key`

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/lab/on-order-paid
Idempotency-Key: SO202610310011-PAID
```

```json
{
  "order_no": "PO202610310011",
  "sales_order_no": "SO202610310011",
  "paid_at": "2026-10-31T09:00:30+08:00",
  "paid_amount": 1280.00,
  "is_urgent": false,
  "items": [
    {
      "order_item_seq": 1,
      "product_code": "OPLN000456",
      "frame_product_code": "OPFR000123",
      "frame_shape": "FULL_RIM",
      "prescription": {
        "eye_side": "OU",
        "sph_od": -4.25, "cyl_od": -0.75, "axis_od": 180,
        "sph_os": -4.00, "cyl_os": -0.50, "axis_os": 175,
        "pd_total": 64.00, "pd_height_od": 22.00, "pd_height_os": 22.00,
        "lens_design": "ASPHERIC"
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "order_no": "PO202610310011",
    "created_count": 1,
    "existed_count": 0,
    "jobs": [
      {
        "lab_job_no": "JG202610310011",
        "order_item_seq": 1,
        "status": "PENDING",
        "template_code": "TP0003",
        "template_name": "渐进片配镜模板",
        "template_match_source": "MDM",
        "step_count": 7,
        "total_minutes": 68,
        "supply_type": "STOCK",
        "expected_piece_at": null,
        "promised_at": "2026-10-31T16:00:00+08:00",
        "planned_finish_at": "2026-10-31T15:42:00+08:00"
      }
    ],
    "idempotent_hit": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑（建单全流程）：**

```
1. 幂等检查
   ├─ 查 m10:idem:lab:{Idempotency-Key}（TTL 24h）
   ├─ 命中 → 直接返回首次结果，idempotent_hit=true
   └─ 未命中 → 继续

2. 逐行处理 items
   ├─ 按 (order_no, order_item_seq, rework_count=1) 查是否已建单
   │   ├─ 已存在 → existed_count+1，跳过（uk_m10_lab_job_order_item 兜底）
   │   └─ 不存在 → 继续
   ├─ 调 M01-08 API-50 匹配工序模板
   │   GET /internal/v1/optical/process-templates/match
   │       ?product_code=OPLN000456&frame_shape=FULL_RIM
   │   ├─ 成功 → template_code/steps 记入
   │   └─ 失败重试 3 次 → 仍失败 → 建 PENDING 单，
   │       template_match_source='FALLBACK'，template_code=NULL
   │       （加工主管在 P03 人工指定，见 §2.4 降级策略）
   ├─ 取供货类型与来片周期
   │   GET /internal/v1/optical/products/{code}/full-config
   ├─ 生成 lab_job_no（Redis INCR m10:code:JG:{yyyyMMdd}）
   ├─ 计算 planned_finish_at / promised_at（§3.4.2）
   ├─ INSERT m10_lab_job（status='PENDING'）
   └─ INSERT m10_lab_job_step × N（status：seq=1 → READY，其余 PENDING）

3. 预占库存（可选，视配置）
   ├─ 若订单含现货镜架 → 调内部 P-19 预占
   └─ 预占失败不回滚建单（建单不需要库存承诺），返回 warnings

4. 发布事件
   ├─ m10.lab.job.created（每个新建的加工单一条）
   └─ 删除 m10:lab:board:{store_id} 缓存

5. 写幂等键 m10:idem:lab:{key} = 响应体快照，TTL 24h
```

**为什么建单不依赖预占成功：** 建单是「生产任务」的登记，预占是「销售承诺」。顾客已付款，生产必须开始；若因库存不足而不建单，会出现「钱收了、单没建」的坏账风险。库存不足时走定制（`supply_type='CUSTOM'`）或调拨，由加工主管跟进。

#### API-57 P-18 查询可销售量

> **契约编号 P-18，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `GET /internal/v1/m10/stock/available`
> **调用方：** M16-04（开单）、M01-08（库存回填）、M10-05（选品工作台）｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 80ms

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/stock/available?store_id=1
    &sku_keys=OPLN000456|-4.25|-0.75|180&sku_keys=OPFR000123|-|-|BLK
    &biz_type=RETAIL&include_custom=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "store_id": 1,
    "queried_at": "2026-10-31T09:15:22+08:00",
    "calc_formula": "AV_ON_HAND_MINUS_RESERVED_MINUS_SAFETY",
    "items": [
      {
        "sku_key": "OPLN000456｜-4.25｜-0.75｜180",
        "product_code": "OPLN000456",
        "product_name": "依视路钻晶A4 1.67 非球面",
        "store_id": 1,
        "qty_on_hand": 3,
        "qty_reserved": 2,
        "qty_safety": 1,
        "qty_available": 0,
        "is_available": false,
        "supply_type": "STOCK",
        "lead_time_days": null,
        "batches": []
      },
      {
        "sku_key": "OPFR000123｜-｜-｜BLK",
        "product_code": "OPFR000123",
        "product_name": "暴龙 BJ7001 钛合金 黑",
        "store_id": 1,
        "qty_on_hand": 2,
        "qty_reserved": 0,
        "qty_safety": 0,
        "qty_available": 2,
        "is_available": true,
        "supply_type": "STOCK",
        "lead_time_days": null,
        "batches": [
          {
            "batch_id": 10388,
            "batch_key": "S01-OPFR000123--|-|-BLK-IN20261020",
            "batch_no": "IN20261020",
            "qty_on_hand": 2,
            "qty_reserved": 0,
            "qty_safety": 0,
            "qty_available": 2,
            "unit": "副",
            "expiry_date": null,
            "status": "AVAILABLE"
          }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "missing_sku_keys": [],
    "cache_hit": true
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**返回值定义（写死，不可含糊）：**

```
qty_available = GREATEST( qty_on_hand − qty_reserved − qty_safety, 0 )
                （单批次生成列，SKU 级为各批次求和）

其中：
  qty_on_hand   门店在架实物数量（**不是**全院物理库存）
  qty_reserved  有效预占量（m10_stock_reserve 中 status='ACTIVE'）
  qty_safety    安全库存（m10_stock_policy 三级继承后冗余到批次）
```

**「物理库存」与「可销售量」的接口对应（必须区分）：**

| 口径 | 接口 | 归属 | 语义 | 典型调用方 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **物理库存（全院）** | `GET /internal/v1/m15/stock/available` | **M15-01** | 主库在库 + 门店二级库在架 | 盘点、资产报表、采购补货 |
| **可销售量（门店）** | `GET /internal/v1/m10/stock/available`（本接口） | **M10-07** | 门店在架 − 已预占 − 安全库存 | M16-04 开单、M10-05 选品 |

> `calc_formula` 字段固定返回 `"AV_ON_HAND_MINUS_RESERVED_MINUS_SAFETY"`，供调用方断言语义，防止误用为物理库存。

**业务逻辑：** 1) 入参 `sku_keys` 最多 100 个，去重后查询；2) 命中 `idx_m10_stock_batch_sku`（部分索引，仅 `AVAILABLE` 行）；3) 按 `store_id + sku_key` 聚合 `SUM(qty_available)`；4) 无批次的 SKU 放入 `missing_sku_keys`；5) Redis 缓存 `m10:stock:avail:{store_id}:{hash(sku_keys)}` TTL 30s；6) `include_custom=true` 时对无现货 SKU 补 `supply_type='CUSTOM'` 与 `lead_time_days`（来自 M01-08 缓存），`is_available` 恒为 true 但带周期。

#### API-58 P-19 预占库存（★事务两段式第一段）

> **契约编号 P-19，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/reserve`
> **调用方：** M16-04 ｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 150ms ｜ **幂等：** 必须带 `Idempotency-Key`

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/reserve
Idempotency-Key: PO202610310011-RESERVE
```

```json
{
  "store_id": 1,
  "source_type": "SALES_ORDER",

  "source_id": 80011,

  "is_urgent": false,
  "items": [
    {
      "sku_key": "OPLN000456｜-4.25｜-0.75｜180",
      "product_code": "OPLN000456",
      "qty": 1,
      "source_item_seq": 1,
      "batch_id": null
    },
    {
      "sku_key": "OPFR000123｜-｜-｜BLK",
      "product_code": "OPFR000123",
      "qty": 1,
      "source_item_seq": 2,
      "batch_id": null
    }
  ]
}
```

**响应（全部成功）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "source_no": "PO202610310011",
    "all_reserved": true,

      }
    ],

  },
```

**响应（部分失败 —— 全量回滚）**

```json
{
  "code": "1007011",
  "message": "可销售量不足，该 SKU 当前可售 0 副",
  "data": {
    "source_no": "PO202610310012",
    "all_reserved": false,
    "success_count": 0,
    "fail_count": 1,
    "reserved": [],
    "failures": [
      {
        "sku_key": "OPLN000456｜-4.25｜-0.75｜180",
        "code": "1007011",
        "message": "可销售量不足：需求 1 副，可售 0 副",
        "qty_requested": 1,
        "qty_available": 0,
        "suggest": "CUSTOM_ORDER",
        "lead_time_days": 7
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑（事务两段式的第一段，逐行在单事务内）：**

```
BEGIN;

-- ① 幂等检查
SELECT * FROM m10_stock_reserve WHERE idempotency_key = :key;
  命中 → 返回首次结果（idempotent）

-- ② 逐行处理 items（按 sku_key 排序，防死锁）
FOR EACH item IN items ORDER BY sku_key:
    -- ③ 锁定批次行（SKIP LOCKED 避免锁等待）
    SELECT id, qty_on_hand, qty_reserved, qty_safety, qty_available, version
    FROM m10_stock_batch
    WHERE store_id = :store_id
      AND sku_key = :sku_key
      AND status = 'AVAILABLE'
      AND is_deleted = FALSE
      AND (batch_id IS NULL OR id = :batch_id)
    ORDER BY expiry_date NULLS LAST, created_at ASC   -- FEFO
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED;

    IF 无行 THEN
        ROLLBACK;  返回 "1007012"（无批次行）
    END IF;

    -- ④ 校验可售量
    IF SUM(qty_available) < item.qty THEN
        ROLLBACK;  返回 "1007011"（可售不足，附 suggest）
    END IF;

    -- ⑤ 分配批次（FEFO 逐批扣减可售份额）
    选定批次 = 第一个 qty_available >= item.qty 的行
              （否则跨批次拆分，但本子模块限制一 SKU 一预占行，故要求单批次足够）

    -- ⑥ 预占 TTL（按策略三级继承 + 加急/定制区分）
    ttl = CASE
            WHEN supply_type = 'CUSTOM' THEN policy.reserve_ttl_custom_minutes
            WHEN is_urgent              THEN policy.reserve_ttl_urgent_minutes
            ELSE policy.reserve_ttl_minutes
          END

    -- ⑦ 写预占行（uk_m10_stock_reserve_active 防重复）
    INSERT INTO m10_stock_reserve (...)
    VALUES (reserve_no, batch_id, qty_reserved, status='ACTIVE',
            reserved_at=now(), expires_at=now()+ttl, idempotency_key,
            release_reason=NULL);

    -- ⑧ 回写台账预占数（生成列 qty_available 自动重算）
    UPDATE m10_stock_batch
    SET qty_reserved = qty_reserved + item.qty,
        version      = version + 1
    WHERE id = 批次.id;
END FOR;

COMMIT;
```

**并发场景：两人同时抢门店最后 1 副**

```
时刻   事务 A（收银员甲 / 顾客甲）                    事务 B（收银员乙 / 顾客乙）
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
T1     POST P-19  reserve(sku_key=S, qty=1)
T2     BEGIN
T3     SELECT ... FROM m10_stock_batch
         WHERE store_id=1 AND sku_key=S AND status='AVAILABLE'
         FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED        ← A 拿到行锁
T4                                                   BEGIN
T5                                                   SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
                                                     ← 该行已被 A 锁住，
                                                       SKIP LOCKED 使其跳过 → 返回 0 行
T6     校验 qty_available = 1 ≥ 1 ✓
T7     INSERT m10_stock_reserve(ACTIVE, qty=1)
T8     UPDATE m10_stock_batch SET qty_reserved=1
T9     COMMIT  ← 释放行锁
T10    返回 { all_reserved:true, reserve_no:YZ... }
T11                                                  无行 → 返回 "1007012"
                                                     前端提示「门店无此 SKU 在架，
                                                     是否改为定制（7 天）」
──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

【变体：若事务 B 在 A 提交后才执行（非同时）】
T11'   B 重新 SELECT（新快照）→ 读到 qty_on_hand=1, qty_reserved=1
T12'   qty_available = GREATEST(1 − 1 − 0, 0) = 0 < 1
T13'   ROLLBACK
T14'   返回 "1007011" 可销售量不足，suggest='CUSTOM_ORDER'

【为什么用 SKIP LOCKED 而不是 FOR UPDATE】
  FOR UPDATE 会让 B 阻塞等待 A 提交（默认 5s 超时）。在门市高峰
  （60 单/小时 × 2 收银台）会产生大量锁等待，把 P-19 的 P95 从 150ms
  推到数秒。SKIP LOCKED 让 B 立即跳过被锁行：多批次时换一个批次，
  单批次时快速失败走定制分支。宁可让 B 走定制，也不要阻塞收银台。

【为什么两段式（预占 + 出库分开）】
  ① 预占只改 qty_reserved，不动 qty_on_hand（实物仍在货架，顾客可能试戴调整）
  ② 付款/交付才产生 OUT_SALE 流水，真正减 qty_on_hand，预占转 CONSUMED
  ③ 中间任一步失败只需 P-20 释放，实物从未离架
  合为一段（下单一并扣减）的问题是：顾客反悔需"负出库倒冲"，而库存流水
  只增不改（见数据结构 4.4），倒冲会污染流水语义与审计链。
```

**预占 TTL 自动释放机制：**

```
APScheduler job_m10_reserve_expire（每分钟）
  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ 1. SELECT reserve_no, batch_id, sku_key, qty_reserved        │
  │    FROM m10_stock_reserve                                    │
  │    WHERE status='ACTIVE' AND expires_at <= now()              │
  │    ORDER BY expires_at LIMIT 500                             │
  │    （命中 idx_m10_stock_reserve_expire 部分索引）              │
  │                                                              │
  │ 2. 逐条在独立事务内：                                          │
  │    BEGIN                                                     │
  │      UPDATE m10_stock_reserve                                │
  │      SET status='EXPIRED', released_at=now(),                 │
  │          release_reason='TTL_EXPIRED'                        │
  │      WHERE reserve_no=? AND status='ACTIVE';                  │
  │      -- rowcount=0 说明已被其他路径处理，跳过                   │
  │                                                              │
  │      UPDATE m10_stock_batch                                  │
  │      SET qty_reserved = GREATEST(qty_reserved − qty, 0),      │
  │          version = version + 1                               │
  │      WHERE id=?;                                             │
  │    COMMIT                                                    │
  │                                                              │
  │ 3. 批量删除缓存 m10:stock:avail:{store_id}:*                  │
  │ 4. 发布事件 m10.stock.reserve.expired（供 M10-06 提示顾客）    │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

TTL 取值（来自 m10_stock_policy 三级继承）：
  普通单  24 小时（1440 分钟）——覆盖「今天开单明天来取」的常见场景
  加急单  72 小时（4320 分钟）——顾客出差/次日取镜
  定制单  7 天  （10080 分钟，上限）——等厂家来片期间必须保留镜架
  上限 7 天由 CHECK 约束强制（ck_m10_stock_reserve_ttl）

误释放的补救：
  若顾客在预占过期后才付款，P-19 会重新预占；若此时已无库存，
  返回 "1007011" 并提示改定制，M16-04 负责通知顾客与改期。
```

**失败错误码汇总（P-19）：**

| 场景 | 错误码 | HTTP | 处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 可售量不足 | `"1007011"` | 200 | 整体回滚，返回 `suggest` 建议 |
| 门店无此 SKU 批次 | `"1007012"` | 200 | 整体回滚，建议调拨/定制 |
| 指定批次被锁定 | `"1007013"` | 409 | 提示换批次重试 |
| 批次已过期/破损 | `"1007016"` | 422 | 阻断，返回可用批次 |
| 批次已冻结 | `"1007020"` | 422 | 阻断，产品停用/召回 |
| 乐观锁冲突 | `"1007015"` | 409 | 提示刷新重试 |
| 幂等键重复 | `0` | 200 | 返回首次结果，不重复占用 |
| SKU 键格式非法 | `"1007010"` | 400 | 参数校验 |
| 请求内 SKU 重复 | `40001` | 400 | 参数校验 |

#### API-59 P-20 释放预占（★事务两段式第二段）

> **契约编号 P-20，路径与共享规范 §8 一字不差：** `POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/release`
> **调用方：** M16-04 ｜ **SLA：** P95 ≤ 150ms ｜ **幂等：** 必须带 `Idempotency-Key`

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/stock/release
Idempotency-Key: PO202610310011-RELEASE
```

```json
{
  "source_no": "PO202610310011",
  "source_type": "SALES_ORDER",
  "reserve_nos": null,
  "release_reason": "ORDER_CANCELLED",
  "remark": "顾客取消订单，已办理退款"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "source_no": "PO202610310011",
    "released_count": 2,
    "released_qty_total": 2,
    "reserve_nos": ["YZ20261031000012", "YZ20261031000013"],
    "skipped": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（幂等重放 / 已释放）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "source_no": "PO202610310011",
    "released_count": 0,
    "released_qty_total": 0,
    "reserve_nos": [],
    "skipped": [
      { "reserve_no": "YZ20261031000012", "reason": "ALREADY_RELEASED" },
      { "reserve_no": "YZ20261031000013", "reason": "ALREADY_EXPIRED" }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：**

```
BEGIN;

-- ① 幂等检查（同上）
-- ② 定位预占
SELECT reserve_no, batch_id, qty_reserved, status
FROM m10_stock_reserve
WHERE source_no = :source_no
  AND (:source_type IS NULL OR source_type = :source_type)
  AND (:reserve_nos IS NULL OR reserve_no = ANY(:reserve_nos))
  AND status IN ('ACTIVE', 'CONSUMED', 'RELEASED', 'EXPIRED')
ORDER BY reserve_no
FOR UPDATE;

-- ③ 逐条处理
FOR EACH r IN rows:
    IF r.status = 'ACTIVE' THEN
        UPDATE m10_stock_reserve
        SET status='RELEASED', released_at=now(),
            release_reason=:release_reason
        WHERE reserve_no=r.reserve_no AND status='ACTIVE';

        UPDATE m10_stock_batch
        SET qty_reserved = GREATEST(qty_reserved - r.qty_reserved, 0),
            version = version + 1
        WHERE id = r.batch_id;
        -- GREATEST 兜底：即使并发导致台账数被其他路径改小，也不会出现负数
    ELSIF r.status = 'CONSUMED' THEN
        -- 已转实际出库，不可释放（实物已离架）
        加入 skipped：[r.reserve_no, 'ALREADY_CONSUMED']
    ELSE
        加入 skipped：[r.reserve_no, r.status]   -- 幂等：重复释放无副作用
    END IF;
END FOR;

COMMIT;
-- 删除缓存 m10:stock:avail:{store_id}:*
```

**三种释放路径的差异：**

| 触发路径 | `release_reason` | 调用方 | 说明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 订单取消/退款 | `ORDER_CANCELLED` / `REFUNDED` | M16-04 | 最常见，预占转 `RELEASED` |
| TTL 自动到期 | `TTL_EXPIRED` | `job_m10_reserve_expire` | 不经本接口，由定时任务内部处理 |
| 人工释放 | `MANUAL` | P07 库存台账 | 必须指定 `reserve_nos`（防误释放整单） |
| 库存不足让位 | `SHORTAGE` | 补货流程 | 为更紧急的加急单让出库存 |

**为什么用 `GREATEST(qty_reserved − qty, 0)` 而不是直接减：**
预占数与台账 `qty_reserved` 是「事实」与「快照」的关系。若因异常（如手工改数、跨服务重放）导致快照偏小，直接减会产生负数，触发 `ck_m10_stock_batch_qty_nonneg` 约束报错，把一次正常的释放变成 500。`GREATEST(...,0)` 让释放永远成功，偏差由每日 `job_m10_reserve_repair`（02:00）按 4.3 权威值重算修正。

### 6.8 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/stock_lab.py
"""M10-07 视光库存与加工作业 —— 路由层（对外 + 内部契约）"""

from datetime import date
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.stock_lab import (
    LabAssignIn, LabJobListOut, LabJobOut, LabOnOrderPaidIn, LabQcIn,
    LabOutsourceReceiveIn, LabOutsourceSendIn, LabStepCompleteIn, LabStepStartIn,
    StockAvailableOut, StockAvailableQuery, StockCheckCreateIn, StockCheckEnterIn,
    StockReleaseIn, StockReleaseOut, StockReserveIn, StockReserveResultOut,
    StockScrapIn,
)
from app.services.m10.lab_job_service import LabJobService
from app.services.m10.lab_dispatch_service import LabDispatchService
from app.services.m10.lab_qc_service import LabQcService
from app.services.m10.stock_available_service import StockAvailableService
from app.services.m10.stock_reserve_service import StockReserveService
from app.services.m10.stock_check_service import StockCheckService

# 对外路由：加工域（/api/v1/m10/lab）
router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/lab", tags=["M10-07 视光加工"])
# 对外路由：库存域（/api/v1/m10/stock）
stock_router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/stock", tags=["M10-07 视光库存"])
# 内部契约（/internal/v1/m10/lab 与 /internal/v1/m10/stock）
internal_lab_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/lab",
                                tags=["M10-07 内部契约-加工"])
internal_stock_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/stock",
                                  tags=["M10-07 内部契约-库存"])


# ==================== 加工看板 ====================

@router.get("/dashboard", summary="加工看板统计卡片")
async def lab_dashboard(
    store_id: int = Query(..., ge=1, description="门店 ID"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回在制数、各状态分布、加急/逾期数、今日建单与完工数。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.get_dashboard(store_id, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/dashboard/board", summary="加工看板在制单列表")
async def lab_board(
    store_id: int = Query(..., ge=1),
    filter_urgent: bool = Query(False),
    filter_overdue: bool = Query(False),
    product_kind: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按 加急 → 逾期 → 优先级 → 承诺时刻 → 建单时刻 五段式排序。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_board(
        store_id=store_id, filter_urgent=filter_urgent,
        filter_overdue=filter_overdue, product_kind=product_kind,
        operator_id=operator_id, keyword=keyword, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/dashboard/operators", summary="加工师实时负载")
async def lab_operator_load(
    store_id: int = Query(..., ge=1),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按 班次剩余 × 效率系数 × 并行系数 − 已排 − 已完成 计算剩余产能。"""
    svc = LabDispatchService(db)
    data = await svc.get_operator_load(store_id)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 加工单 ====================

@router.get("/jobs", summary="加工单分页列表")
async def list_lab_jobs(
    date_from: date = Query(...),
    date_to: date = Query(...),
    status: Optional[List[str]] = Query(None),
    product_kind: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    operator_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    is_urgent: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    is_overdue: Optional[bool] = Query(None),
    job_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=64),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """加工单列表，日期跨度不得超过 366 天。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    page_result = await svc.list_jobs(
        date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to, status=status,
        product_kind=product_kind, operator_id=operator_id,
        is_urgent=is_urgent, is_overdue=is_overdue, job_type=job_type,
        keyword=keyword, page=page, page_size=page_size,
    )
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/jobs/{lab_job_no}", summary="加工单详情", response_model=None)
async def get_lab_job(
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回加工单主信息 + 全部工序执行明细 + 处方快照。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.get_detail(lab_job_no, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/cancel", summary="取消加工单")
async def cancel_lab_job(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """仅 PENDING/ASSIGNED 可取消，同事务释放全部有效预占。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.cancel(lab_job_no, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/priority", summary="调整优先级与加急标记")
async def set_lab_priority(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """priority 1~9；置加急后按工期模型重算 promised_at。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.set_priority(lab_job_no, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/chain", summary="返工链查询")
async def get_lab_chain(
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按 root_job_no 聚合首次单与全部返工单。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.get_chain(lab_job_no)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 派工 ====================

@router.get("/dispatch/queue", summary="待派工队列")
async def dispatch_queue(
    store_id: int = Query(..., ge=1),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """待派工队列，按派工算法排序。"""
    svc = LabDispatchService(db)
    page_result = await svc.get_queue(store_id, page, page_size)
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.post("/dispatch/assign", summary="派工（单条）")
async def dispatch_assign(
    payload: LabAssignIn,
    request: Request,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """支持整单派工与按工序派工；产能不足需 force=true。"""
    svc = LabDispatchService(db)
    data = await svc.assign(payload, operator=user,
                            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                            trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/dispatch/batch-assign", summary="批量派工")
async def dispatch_batch_assign(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """逐条在独立保存点执行，返回逐条结果。"""
    svc = LabDispatchService(db)
    data = await svc.batch_assign(payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/dispatch/reassign", summary="改派")
async def dispatch_reassign(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:dispatch")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """原派工置 REASSIGNED，新建 ACTIVE 派工；必须填改派原因。"""
    svc = LabDispatchService(db)
    data = await svc.reassign(payload, operator=user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 工序执行 ====================

@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/steps/{step_seq}/start", summary="工序开工")
async def start_step(
    payload: LabStepStartIn,
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    step_seq: int = Path(..., ge=1),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:process")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """校验前置工序全部完成，否则返回 1007031。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.start_step(lab_job_no, step_seq, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/steps/{step_seq}/complete", summary="工序完工报工")
async def complete_step(
    payload: LabStepCompleteIn,
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    step_seq: int = Path(..., ge=1),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:lab:process")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """写实际工时；下一道自动置 READY；最后一道完成则整单转 QC_PENDING。"""
    svc = LabJobService(db)
    data = await svc.complete_step(lab_job_no, step_seq, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== 质检 ====================

@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/qc", summary="提交整单质检")
async def submit_qc(
    payload: LabQcIn,

    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/qc/rework", summary="判定返工")
async def judge_rework(
    payload: dict = Body(...),

    data = await svc.judge_rework(lab_job_no, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/jobs/{lab_job_no}/qc/concession", summary="判定让步接受")
async def judge_concession(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    lab_job_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),

):

    data = await svc.judge_concession(lab_job_no, payload, user)
    return success_response(data)

@stock_router.get("/ledger", summary="门店库存台账（SKU 聚合）")
```
